Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in power theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 688 and the invariant I(A,B) = 595 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_688(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 688. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^595(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the power structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 460. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 626 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 460 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the power structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 626. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
